Use when the user wants to benchmark on nuPlan (navhard subset), or asks about evaluating this task. Reports EPDMS. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pseudo-simulation-eval) · ★3)

- **pseudo-space-reconstruction-from-scrna-seq**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq count matrix and a matched spatial transcriptomics reference from the same tissue type, and need to identify spatially heterogeneous cell subpopulations or pairwise cellular spatial associations from single-cell-only data. - **pseudo-spatial-coordinate-generation-for-single-cell-multiomics**: Use when applying a spatial model (e.g., SMOPCA) to single-cell multi-omics count matrices that have no physical coordinates and you need pseudo-spatial locations derived from the transcriptomic latent space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/pseudo-spatial-coordinate-generation-for-single-cell-multiomics))

- **pseudobulk-differential-expression**: Pseudobulk differential expression analysis using PyDESeq2. Works on any AnnData with raw integer counts in a layer — AVITI24, scRNA-seq, or AnnData built from a CSV count matrix plus sample metadata. Use when the user asks about differential expression, which genes are up or down, comparing gene expression between conditions or treatments, or DE analysis. - **pseudobulk-sample-aggregation**: Use when bulk RNA-seq or simulated tissue samples must be collapsed into pseudobulk profiles and deconvolved against a sketch-derived single-cell reference, with per-sample cell-type proportion estimates compared to known true proportions. - **pseudotime-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when pseudotime orderings from redPATH and baseline methods must be quantitatively compared on the same filtered expression matrix against known cell type/stage labels using CI, BI, KC, and POS scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-benchmark-evaluation))
- **pseudotime-benchmarking-on-real-datasets**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset has a known number of branches and cell-type ordering (e.g., CMP GSE72857 with eight branches, HSMM with two, MEF with three) and you must choose which TI method to trust before pseudotime-based downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-benchmarking-on-real-datasets))
- **pseudotime-binned-pal-averaging**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data has a cell pseudotime/trajectory ordering (e.g., monocle3) and the goal is to identify which MSigDB KEGG/BIOCARTA pathways are coordinated and active at successive developmental stages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-binned-pal-averaging))
- **pseudotime-branch-visualization**: Use when scRNA-seq cells from a new condition (e.g., Gfi1-/-, Irf8-/-, double-knockout GMPs) must be projected onto an existing wild-type branching trajectory to read off branch-level fate allocation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-branch-visualization))
- **pseudotime-branching-assignment**: Use when you have run CellPath's all_in_one() on scRNA-seq data with scVelo-computed velocities and need per-cell pseudotime and branching assignments to identify lineage paths (e.g., α/β/δ endocrine fates) or run path-level differential expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-branching-assignment))
- **pseudotime-capture-time-correlation**: Use when a pseudotime method has been fit on scRNA-seq data whose cells have known physical capture/sampling times, and you need an external check that the latent ordering recapitulates real time progression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-capture-time-correlation))
- **pseudotime-cell-ordering**: Use when you have a merged, quantile-normalized scRNA-seq matrix spanning sequential developmental stages (e.g. pre-implantation mouse embryos) and need cells ordered along pseudotime for trajectory-based or temporal-communication analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-cell-ordering))
- **pseudotime-cell-type-correlation-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq data has a known biological ordering of cell types along a lineage and you need to score pseudotime from Cytopath or baselines (Slingshot, Monocle3, PAGA, VeTra, Cellpath) against that ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-cell-type-correlation-analysis))
- **pseudotime-concordance-metric-computation**: Use when you have per-cell pseudotime and fate-probability values from two trajectory inference implementations run on the same single-cell VDJ dataset and need quantitative concordance metrics to benchmark them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-concordance-metric-computation))
- **pseudotime-correlated-gene-heatmap-generation**: Use when pseudotime trajectories are inferred for a CellexalVR cell selection (e.g. slingshot on four clusters) and a pseudotime-ordered heatmap of correlated marker genes with ten colour groups is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-correlated-gene-heatmap-generation))
- **pseudotime-correlation-analysis**: Use when a hyperbolic scPhere embedding of a time-course single-cell dataset (e.g., C. elegans embryo GSE126954, 86,024 cells) must be checked for whether latent geometry encodes progression time as a pseudotime signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-correlation-analysis--03d8603e0f44300e))
- **pseudotime-correlation-analysis**: Use when benchmarking feature selection on simulated or labeled trajectory data where ground-truth latent progression (e.g., Splatter step vector, SymSim latent time) is available and pseudotime fidelity must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-correlation-analysis--986cd07af769d7ae))
- **pseudotime-correlation-assessment**: Use when benchmarking a trajectory inference method on dyntoy synthetic datasets where gold-standard pseudotime and cell-fate labels are known and you must quantify agreement of inferred pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-correlation-assessment))
- **pseudotime-correlation-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking trajectory inference methods on simulated single-cell RNA-seq data with known gold-standard pseudotime and branch labels, especially when quantifying how technical noise (sigma) or capture efficiency (alpha) affects method performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-correlation-evaluation))
- **pseudotime-dag-construction**: Use when single-cell multimodal (paired RNA-seq + ATAC-seq) trajectories need a cell-state partial ordering — not a total pseudotime — to support lagged message-passing causal inference over peak-gene pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-dag-construction))
- **pseudotime-gene-expression-correlation-analysis**: Use when comparing trajectory-inference algorithms that each output a pseudotime ordering of the same scRNA-seq cells and you need a quantitative, gene-centric smoothness metric to rank them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-gene-expression-correlation-analysis))
- **pseudotime-gene-expression-kinetics-comparison**: Use when you need to ask whether cell-type marker gene expression dynamics are conserved or divergent between species/conditions along branched pseudotime trajectories, without manually selecting linear lineage paths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-gene-expression-kinetics-comparison))
- **pseudotime-gene-expression-track-visualization**: Use when a supervised LDA embedding yields a circular or ordered cell arrangement (e.g., cell-cycle phases on LD axes) and you need to confirm phase ordering by tracking marker expression (e.g., CCNB1, CDK1, TPX2) along the inferred pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-gene-expression-track-visualization))
- **pseudotime-gene-expression-trend-visualization**: Use when an SSGATE-integrated spatial AnnData with a Leiden clustering exists and the goal is to verify a spatial developmental trajectory (e.g., outer cortex to inner medulla) and its gene expression dynamics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/pseudotime-gene-expression-trend-visualization))
- **pseudotime-kendall-correlation-evaluation**: Use when evaluating inferred cell orderings on simulated single-cell RNA-seq data with known ground-truth pseudotime and branching structure, to rank TI methods (CellPath, VeTra, Slingshot, CellRank, Vdpt) by per-path Kendall correlation and assignment entropy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-kendall-correlation-evaluation))
- **pseudotime-lineage-fitting**: Use when single-cell data from multiple conditions has been embedded (e.g., Dim1/Dim2 reduced-dimension coordinates with cluster and condition labels) and you must decide whether to fit one common trajectory or test for differential topology and differential progression between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-lineage-fitting))
- **pseudotime-lineage-preprocessing-and-filtering**: Use when preparing multi-sample scRNA-seq data (e.g., HCA bone marrow counts with cell-to-sample annotation and Slingshot/TSCAN pseudotime) before running lamian_test() for trajectory differential expression or density testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-lineage-preprocessing-and-filtering))
- **pseudotime-marker-gene-correlation-analysis**: Use when comparing trajectory inference methods on the HSMM scRNA-seq dataset, or when quantifying pseudotime accuracy via Pearson/Spearman/Kendall correlation against known marker genes such as ENO3, MEF2C, MYH2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-marker-gene-correlation-analysis))
- **pseudotime-method-benchmarking**: Use when comparing how pseudotime algorithms degrade as the fraction of genes with covariate-pseudotime interactions increases, or when validating a new trajectory method against standard ones on simulated data with a known latent time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-method-benchmarking))
- **pseudotime-noise-injection**: Use when you need to quantify how sensitive a trajectory-level differential progression test (e.g., condiments progressionTest) is to imperfect pseudotime estimates or incorrect cell-to-lineage assignments from upstream trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-noise-injection))
- **pseudotime-normalization-single-cell**: Use when single-cell expression data has an ordering along an inferred trajectory and must be formatted (ptime array, 0-100 rescale, sparse X, optional branches/regix) before running SINGE GLG. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-normalization-single-cell))
- **pseudotime-ordered-expression-extraction**: Use when time-course scRNA-seq data have been trajectory-ordered (e.g. monocle3 pseudotime) and you need aligned per-gene expression series along pseudotime to test ligand-receptor co-variation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-ordered-expression-extraction))
- **pseudotime-ordering-with-monocle-and-scuba**: Decision support for choosing a trajectory-inference method when a scRNA-seq progenitor dataset is suspected to contain a branching event and single-path pseudotime methods (SCUBA, monocle) may miss it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-ordering-with-monocle-and-scuba))
- **pseudotime-ordering-with-slingshot**: Use when a processed scRNA-seq dataset (Seurat object with Louvain cluster assignments and PCA/UMAP/PHATE reductions) needs trajectory orientation and per-cell pseudotime values along defined lineages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-ordering-with-slingshot))
- **pseudotime-oriented-trajectory-modeling**: Use when paired single-cell multimodal data (e.g., scRNA-seq + scATAC-seq from the same cells) captures a dynamic process such as differentiation, and a temporally oriented cell-state graph is needed for causal or trajectory modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-oriented-trajectory-modeling))
- **pseudotime-percentile-normalization**: Use when CellPath assigns a cell to more than one trajectory path (cellpath_obj.pseudo_order) and you need to quantify pseudotime consistency across paths per dataset, e.g. reproducing Figure S4G Diffpst category proportions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-percentile-normalization))
- **pseudotime-perturbation-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when ordered single-cell expression data are available and you need to test whether inferred or assigned pseudotemporal values (rather than the expression matrix itself) drive the quality of pseudotime-based network inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-perturbation-sensitivity-analysis))
- **pseudotime-projection-to-single-cells**: Use when a miloR pseudobulk object carries pseudotime and branch probabilities computed in VDJ feature space, and per-cell values are needed for plotting, downstream analysis, or benchmarking against Palantir. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-projection-to-single-cells))
- **pseudotime-sample-ranking**: Use when you have per-sample cell-type/structure cluster distributions (e.g. pathomics .h5ad with clusters_col='Cell_type', sample_col='sampleID', status='status') and need an unsupervised ordering of patients along disease progression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-sample-ranking))
- **pseudotime-scoring-with-tscan-pos-kendall**: Use when comparing scRNA-seq imputation methods by how well each imputed matrix recovers cell-to-cell trajectory order — e.g., scoring imputed 10x data with TSCAN POS and Kendall rank correlation to rank Bfimpute vs. competitors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-scoring-with-tscan-pos-kendall))
- **pseudotime-similarity-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking cell lineage construction from time-course scRNA-seq: you have cells sampled at adjacent time points and want to quantify whether aligned trajectories show higher cell-to-cell similarity (smaller pseudo-time changes) than a baseline alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-similarity-evaluation))
- **pseudotime-spearman-correlation-analysis**: Use when a trajectory/pseudotime method has been run on scRNA-seq data with a known cell type ordering and you must quantify agreement per lineage and check consistency across multiple independent runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-spearman-correlation-analysis))
- **pseudotime-stimulation-duration-correlation**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset carries a known per-cell elapsed-time label (e.g. stimulation minutes) and you need to test whether velocity-derived pseudotime tracks biological process time, ideally against a no-velocity ablation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-stimulation-duration-correlation))
- **pseudotime-to-spatial-scale-transformation**: Use when single-cell flow cytometry data of radially symmetric multicellular spheroids have been ordered in pseudotime and marker profiles (Ki-67, p27, DNA, RNA) must be recovered versus distance from the spheroid surface. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-to-spatial-scale-transformation))
- **pseudotime-to-vector-field-conversion**: Use when in silico TF perturbation simulations (e.g., Gata1 KO on the Paul et al. 2015 hematopoiesis data) must be quantified as scores by comparing simulated transitions to the pseudotime differentiation gradient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-to-vector-field-conversion))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq cells have a pseudotime/trajectory ordering (e.g. monocle3 on GSE153162 E17.5 mouse cortex) and the goal is to find which pathway gene sets are active at each developmental stage and how their activity curves cluster over pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-analysis))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-benchmarking**: Use when a splicing-free velocity or trajectory method must be validated against a multi-omics baseline: compare pseudotime ordering, streamplot direction, and gene phase-portrait dynamics on a shared dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-benchmarking))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-correlation**: Use when comparing two single-cell cohorts (e.g., AI-generated/semi-profiled vs. real-profiled) to verify that developmental or disease-progression trajectories are preserved, via Monocle3 pseudotime computed on shared UMAP coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-correlation))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq captures a continuous biological transition (e.g. naive CD4+ T cell activation through intermediate populations at 0 h, 12 h, 24 h) and you need pathway dynamics along the trajectory rather than a two-group comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-inference--3a6c808d9f5bd99d))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-inference**: Use when single-cell RNA-Seq data from two or more conditions must be placed on a common trajectory to ask whether conditions differ in topology, pseudotime progression, fate selection between lineages, or gene expression along lineages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-inference--8cc898bea49663cb))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-inference**: Use when a single-cell multi-omic dataset has been co-embedded, batch-corrected (RunHarmony) and sub-clustered, and the goal is to order clusters along a differentiation continuum (e.g. progenitors to myofibroblasts) for GRN inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-inference--72767d512e6640bb))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-inference**: Use when dropout-affected scRNA-seq data has been imputed (e.g., with SERM vs. MAGIC) and the goal is to verify that developmental trajectories and state transitions are recovered, not just gene values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-inference--b314d5705a09022d))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq captures a continuous state-transition process (e.g., acinar-to-ductal transdifferentiation) and you need a pseudotime-anchored reference direction to benchmark velocity vectors against, as in the ADM-axis concordance task. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-inference--4956ca65b2302310))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-inference**: Use when you have single-cell counts with known developmental/staged time labels (e.g., Chu GSE75748, Scialdone GSE74994, Petropoulos E-MTAB-3929) and need to test whether imputation recovers true temporal cell order. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-inference--9f79e2a527183213))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-inference-slingshot**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq dataset (e.g. GSE81682 mouse HSPC from Nestorowa et al., 2016) must be ordered along a pseudotime trajectory with slingshot and visualized as group-wise colour groups in CellexalVR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-inference-slingshot))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-inference-visualization**: Use when evaluating whether pseudotime-based approaches such as TSCAN can identify differentiation pathways on single-cell RNA-seq data given the same number of clusters as a class-informed method, or when comparing trajectory inference to visualization baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-inference-visualization))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-inference-with-scfates**: Use when you have SiFT-filtered AnnData expression (e.g. local spatial signal removed via a coordinate-based kernel) and need to order cells along a trajectory and split genes into early/late pseudotime groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-inference-with-scfates))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-signaling-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq cells form continuous pseudotemporal trajectories (e.g. E14.5 embryonic skin dermal/epidermal states) and you need cell-state-specific ligand-receptor signaling rather than cluster-level communication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-signaling-analysis))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-tree-alignment**: Use when two scRNA-seq datasets each have branching pseudotime trajectories (multiple lineages) and you need to match corresponding clusters/lineages across datasets without pre-selecting which single lineages to compare. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-trajectory-tree-alignment))
- **pseudotime-trajectory-viz**: Analyze data with `pseudotime-trajectory-viz` using a reproducible workflow, explicit validation, and structured outputs for review-ready interpretation. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Data%20Analysis/pseudotime-trajectory-viz) · ★2.1k)
- **pseudotime-tree-visualization**: Use when a multi-sample scRNA-seq pseudotime tree has just been inferred (e.g. via infer_tree_structure with origin.marker and number.cluster) and you need to inspect or publish the trajectory's branch structure and cell ordering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-tree-visualization))
- **pseudotime-vs-collection-time-outlier-verification**: Use when a scRNA-seq trajectory inference run shows cells whose pseudotime ordering contradicts their known collection time point, and you want to automatically remove such outliers via fair sampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudotime-vs-collection-time-outlier-verification))
- **pseudouridine-site-fragment-extraction**: Use when constructing a training/testing dataset of RNA fragments centered on known Ψ sites from RMBase coordinates plus reference genome FASTAs, especially to train or benchmark Ψ site classifiers on human, mouse, or yeast data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudouridine-site-fragment-extraction))
- **pseudouridine-site-gene-list-annotation**: Use when you have a list of genes with predicted Ψ sites (e.g., MU-PseUDeep human transcriptome scan at &gt;0.99 precision) and need GO/pathway/disease over-representation against a protein-coding background. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudouridine-site-gene-list-annotation))
- **psexec**: Auth/lab ref: Impacket psexec for remote SYSTEM-level shell execution on Windows hosts via SMB. ([AeonDave/malskill](https://github.com/AeonDave/malskill/tree/HEAD/offensive-tools/windows/psexec) · ★42)
- **psexec-lateral-movement**: Execute commands and binaries on remote Windows systems utilizing PsExec and SMB/RPC mechanisms. This skill details the mechanics behind tools like Sysinternals PsExec, Impacket's psexec.py, and their role in lateral movement via hidden administrative shares. ([ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge](https://github.com/ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge/tree/HEAD/skills/curated/psexec-lateral-movement) · ★27 · 3 copies)
